### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- `eval_grpo_g8_bf16` cron 回调 → 汇总 ckpt80/90/100 metrics → 选最佳 checkpoint → 与 QLoRA GRPO (0.2167) 对比确认 forgetting 不复现 → 更新 registry + memory → 汇报 Director（C2 re-scope 最终版）
- `eval_plan_001_flat_nli` cron 回调 → flat-NLI rel_f1 与 CED RSFT (0.4596) 对比 → 差距 <1pp 则 CED margin 无增益（影响 paper 对 CED 的 claim），差距 >2pp 则 CED margin 关键 → 汇报
- `eval_plan_014_sft_no_evidence` cron 回调 → SFT-no-evidence rel_f1 vs SFT (0.4057) → 如果大幅高于 SFT 则 evidence generation 是 rel_f1 gap 主因（解释 vs LMRC 0.607 的差距）→ 汇报
- `worker_eval_plan_012_dpo_ced` 完成 → 检查 DPO eval 是否已提交到 cuda:2 → 如未提交则立即提交
- `worker_research_quantization-rl-interaction` 完成 → 文献结果入 literature_map → 强化 C2 claim 的 related work
- 所有 3 个 eval 完成后 → 全景汇报 Director → 推进 paper reframe（retitle + related work 扩展 + forgetting figure）
- cuda:2 持续空闲 → 立即提交 plan_012 DPO eval 或 plan_009 forgetting figure 数据准备

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**投稿目标**：EMNLP 2026 Main, ARR July cycle (~7月15-25)，剩余 ~2 个月。

**核心发现链**：
- D031: GRPO+QLoRA catastrophic forgetting（全 3 种 reward type 崩塌）→ pivot 到 RSFT
- D038: RSFT 实际成功（之前是 eval bug 误判）→ 3-seed mean rel_f1=0.4596 (+5.39pp vs SFT)
- D051: bf16 GRPO 无 forgetting (rel_f1=0.4549) → forgetting 是 QLoRA-specific
- D052/D053: plan_011 vs mvp2_grpo_ced_kl001_r4 已构成 clean quantization ablation → plan_015 killed

**论文定位**（Dream v2 后 reframe）：从 CED-centric 转向 "task definition + decoupling finding + EDCR metric"。Decoupling 发现（rel_f1↑但 EDCR↓）是最强 novelty point。CED 降为 "one scoring option"。

**Dream v2 未达标**（best=3.0 < 3.5）：5 个结构性问题待解——(1) CED≈flat-NLI 标题错配 (2) QLoRA confound (3) related work 缺口 (4) 缺 forgetting figure (5) DPO 细节不足。当前 3 个 eval 正在填补 (1)(2)(4)(5)。

**最容易判断错的事**：
- plan_011_eval_g8_bf16_ckpt100（PREPARING）与 eval_grpo_g8_bf16（RUNNING）是重叠的——后者已包含 ckpt100，前者可废弃
- EDCR 下降不是 bug 而是 recall-oriented training 的固有 trade-off（pred_count 增加 → 更多 distractor citation）
- CED reranker (0.4864) 是 best rel_f1 但 8x 推理成本，论文中定位为 upper-bound reference 而非主推方案
- inference.py 的 `--no_evidence` flag（无下划线连字符）用于 SFT-no-evidence eval，与 `--no-quantize`（有连字符）拼写风格不同

{
  "parentUuid": "864d8a91-bd3c-4c56-b21d-e663c64afcac",

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50

  "task_type": "analysis:plan_009_forgetting_figure",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：plan_009 — GRPO Forgetting Dynamics Figure\n\n### 目标\n整理全部 GRPO checkpoint 的 rel_f1/evi_f1/EDCR/reward 轨迹，绘制 training dynamics 可视化图。这是论文核心 figure，展示 QLoRA GRPO catastrophic forgetting vs bf16 GRPO 正常训练的对比。\n\n### 背景\n- **QLoRA GRPO (G=4)** 全线 catastrophic forgetting：rel_f1 从 SFT baseline 0.4057 暴跌到 &lt;0.05 或 max 0.2167\n- **bf16 GRPO (G=8, plan_011)** 无 forgetting：rel_f1=0.4549，甚至超过 SFT baseline\n- 这个对比是论文 Claim 3（decoupling finding 是 QLoRA-specific）的核心视觉证据\n\n### 数据来源\n\n**1. QLoRA GRPO runs（W&B 或 registry metrics）**:\n- `mvp2_grpo_ced_kl001_r4`: 最佳 QLoRA run, rel_f1=0.2167 (dev eval), W&B 有 training reward 轨迹\n- `mvp2_grpo_ced`: CED reward, G=4\n- `mvp2_grpo_flatnli`: flat-NLI reward, G=4\n- `mvp2_grpo_extract`: extract reward, G=4\n- 各种 lr 变体（lr5e-5, lr5e-6）\n\n**2. bf16 GRPO (plan_011)**:\n- `plan_011_grpo_g8_bf16`: G=8, bf16 LoRA, 训练成功\n- `plan_011_grpo_g8_bf16_eval`: full dev eval, rel_f1=0.4549\n- `eval_grpo_g8_bf16`: ckpt80 rel_f1=0.4456, ckpt90/100 待确认\n\n**3. SFT baseline**: rel_f1=0.4057 (水平参考线)\n\n### 具体步骤\n\n1. **数据收集**（优先 W&B，回退 registry/logs）:\n   - 先读 registry 获取所有 GRPO 相关实验的 metrics 和 wandb_run_id\n   - 用 `fetch_wandb_metrics` 获取 W&B 上的 training step-level metrics（reward, loss, grad_norm 等）\n   - 如果 W&B 没有 step-level eval metrics，从服务器日志中提取 checkpoint-level eval results\n   - SSH 到 rtx5090-4x 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/` 下各 GRPO 输出目录的 eval 结果文件\n\n2. **数据整理**:\n   - 创建一个 CSV/JSON 汇总表，columns: exp_id, quantization(QLoRA/bf16), G(4/8), reward_type(CED/flatNLI/extract), step, rel_f1, evi_f1, edcr, reward\n   - SFT baseline 作为 step=0 的参考点\n\n3. **画图**（读 `.claude/skills/draw-paper-figure.skill.md` 获取画图规范）:\n   - Figure A: rel_f1 vs training step, 按 quantization 分色（QLoRA=红系, bf16=蓝系），SFT baseline 水平虚线\n   - Figure B: evi_f1 vs training step, 同样分色\n   - Figure C (可选): EDCR vs training step\n   - 用 subplot 组合成一个 multi-panel figure\n   - 输出 PDF + PNG 到 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/artifacts/`\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: rtx5090-4x\n- Python: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base）\n- W&B project: 从 registry 的 wandb_run_id 字段获取\n- 画图可以在本地（你的进程里）用 matplotlib 执行，也可以 SSH 到服务器执行\n- 产出目录: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/artifacts/`\n\n### 产出\n1. 数据汇总 CSV（存 artifacts/）\n2. forgetting_dynamics.pdf + .png（存 artifacts/）\n3. 用 `register_artifact` 注册产出\n4. 回报图中的关键数据点和 narrative（用于论文引用）",
